# Ashok catalogues import, 2026-10-29

Every candidate this import adds, from `candidates.py digest`. These are all manuscripts known
from Ashok's catalogues, so they list under "Leads", ordered by when each work was composed or
first copied, earliest first. Work titles are OCR'd and lightly corrected; each line's
label keeps the OCR'd title, and its version note gives the catalogue entry and PDF page. Also
listed: the 3 candidates of sweep #331 still awaiting promotion.

To turn something down before merging, comment `reject <id>` on the PR.

### Undated copies

- + **Bhagwata Purana**: Gurmukhi Bhagvat Puran manuscript (Internet Archive, sikhparcharworldwide), ends at the 9th dhiai · Gurmukhi · Panjabi · [link](https://archive.org/details/bhagvat_puran) · `v-gurmukhi-bhagvat-puran-manuscript-internet-archive-sikhparch`
- + **Bishnu Bhagvat da Dasam [Skandh]**: Gurmukhi Bhagvat Puran manuscript, 10th Sakhandh (Internet Archive, sikhparcharworldwide) · Gurmukhi · Panjabi · [link](https://archive.org/details/puratan_pothi) · `v-gurmukhi-bhagvat-puran-manuscript-10th-sakhandh-internet-arc`
